export class CircuitBreaker implements ICircuitBreaker {
    constructor(config?: CircuitBreakerConfig);
    // (undocumented)
    checkCircuit(endpoint: string): void;
    // (undocumented)
    cleanup(): number;
    // (undocumented)
    getState(endpoint: string): CircuitState;
    // (undocumented)
    getStats(): {
        totalCircuits: number;
        openCircuits: number;
        circuits: Record<string, {
            state: CircuitState;
            failures: number;
        }>;
    };
    // (undocumented)
    recordFailure(endpoint: string, statusCode: number): void;
    // (undocumented)
    recordSuccess(endpoint: string): void;
    // (undocumented)
    reset(endpoint?: string): void;
    // (undocumented)
    shutdown(): void;
}

}

// @public (undocumented)
export interface EsiResponse<T> {
    // (undocumented)
    data: T;
    // (undocumented)
    meta: EsiResponseMeta;
}

// @public (undocumented)
function esiResponse<T extends z.ZodTypeAny>(dataSchema: T): z.ZodObject<{
    data: T;
    meta: z.ZodObject<{
        headers: z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodString>;
        fromCache: z.ZodBoolean;
        stale: z.ZodBoolean;
        cacheHitType: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodEnum<{
            "spec-ttl": "spec-ttl";
            "etag-304": "etag-304";
            "stale-on-error": "stale-on-error";
        }>>;
        warning: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodObject<{
            code: z.ZodNumber;
            message: z.ZodString;
        }, z.core.$strip>>;
        requestId: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
        date: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
        contentLanguage: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
        rateLimit: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodObject<{
            remaining: z.ZodNumber;
            limit: z.ZodNumber;
            used: z.ZodNumber;
            group: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
        }, z.core.$strip>>;
        responseTimeMs: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodNumber>;
    }, z.core.$loose>;
}, z.core.$strip>;

// @public (undocumented)
export type EsiResponseMeta = z.infer<typeof EsiResponseMetaSchema>;

// @public (undocumented)
const EsiResponseMetaSchema: z.ZodObject<{
    headers: z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodString>;
    fromCache: z.ZodBoolean;
    stale: z.ZodBoolean;
    cacheHitType: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodEnum<{
        "spec-ttl": "spec-ttl";
        "etag-304": "etag-304";
        "stale-on-error": "stale-on-error";
    }>>;
    warning: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodObject<{
        code: z.ZodNumber;
        message: z.ZodString;
    }, z.core.$strip>>;
    requestId: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
    date: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
    contentLanguage: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
    rateLimit: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodObject<{
        remaining: z.ZodNumber;
        limit: z.ZodNumber;
        used: z.ZodNumber;
        group: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
    }, z.core.$strip>>;
    responseTimeMs: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodNumber>;
}, z.core.$loose>;

// @public (undocumented)
export type EsiResult<T> = {
    ok: true;
    data: T;
    meta: EsiResponseMeta;
} | {
    ok: false;
    error: EsiError;
    meta?: EsiResponseMeta;
};

export interface ETagCacheConfig {
    // (undocumented)
    cleanupInterval?: number;
    // (undocumented)
    defaultTtl?: number;
    // (undocumented)
    maxEntries?: number;
}

// @public (undocumented)
export class ETagCacheManager implements ICache {
    constructor(config?: ETagCacheConfig);
    cleanup(): number;
    clear(): void;
    delete(url: string): boolean;

// @public
export type InferEndpointResult<D> = D extends {
    cursorPagination: true;
} ? D extends {
    responseSchema: infer S extends z.ZodTypeAny;
} ? CursorResult<UnwrapArray<z.infer<S>>> : CursorResult : D extends {
    responseSchema: infer S extends z.ZodTypeAny;
} ? z.infer<S> : unknown;

// @public
export type UnwrapArray<T> = T extends readonly (infer E)[] ? E : T;
